                                     800 *---------------------------------------------------------------------* 00400045
                                     801 *        DSPL3270 UTILIZES A WORK AREA (AT WKAORG) IN AUTOMATIC       * 00408045
                                     802 *        STORAGE WHICH DEFINES THE CURRENT FORMAT OF THE SCREEN.      * 00416045
                                     803 *        THE CONTENT OF THIS AREA IS INITIALIZED ONE OF TWO WAYS,     * 00424045
                                     804 *        DEPENDING OF THE SETTING OF THE RESET/NORESET OPERAND        * 00432045
                                     805 *        CONSIDERED IN CONJUNCTION WITH THE EXISTENCE/NONEXISTENCE    * 00450045
                                     806 *        OF PREVIOUSLY ESTABLISHED DUMP CLUSTER DATA:                 * 00458045
                                     807 *        1. INITIALIZED USING DATA SAVED IN THE DUMP CLUSTER IF       * 00466045
                                     808 *           SUCH DATA EXISTS AND NORESET WAS ENTERED OR ALLOWED TO    * 00474045
                                     809 *           DEFAULT.                                                  * 00482045
                                     810 *        2. INITIALIZED USING PERMANENT VALUES ADDRESSED BY FIELD     * 00500045
                                     811 *           ZZ1327WP IF RESET WAS SPECIFIED AND/OR NO DUMP CLUSTER    * 00508045
                                     812 *           EXISTS FOR THE CURRENT DUMP.                              * 00516045
